# File-organization audit findings — 2026-09-13

Read-only audit of every major junction (14 readers), each stale-claim finding checked by two independent
refuters, then a completeness critic. Source of truth for the nine-PRD "File-organization refresh" chain
(plan: ~/.claude/plans/i-would-like-you-jiggly-pretzel.md). A claim is CONFIRMED when neither refuter could
reproduce the doc as correct; CONTESTED when they split; REFUTED when both found the doc still correct.

Totals: 200 confirmed · 13 contested · 14 refuted · 14 junctions.

This file is a frozen findings record (see reviews/README.md). Line numbers are as of 2026-09-13 and will drift;
grep the quoted text, not the number.

## Critic — top priorities

1. PRIVACY CLAIM IS INVERTED AND SHIPS TO NPM — README.md:11 ("Free, MIT, zero telemetry") and README.md:75 ("Zero telemetry by default") vs CLAUDE.md:128 ("Telemetry is anonymous, on by default, opt-out (SM_TELEMETRY=0)") and src/main/lib/telemetrySettings.cjs:36 (`enabled: true`) + :6 ("ON by default — hard kill switch is SM_TELEMETRY=0"). Verified all four by grep today. README.md is in package.json files[21], so this is a false privacy guarantee on the public npm listing, and it is the one claim where an agent reading the README will confidently tell a user something untrue. Fix the doc (or the default) before anything else in this audit.
2. THE AUDIT'S OWN OUTPUT DOES NOT FIT ITS OWN LAW — 13 of 14 junctions each propose a `root_pointer_line` for root CLAUDE.md. Summed they are ~2,800 characters. `wc -c CLAUDE.md` = 11,991 against the self-declared 12,000-char budget at CLAUDE.md:9, enforced by src/main/health.cjs and re-confirmed by `node scripts/ops-sweep.cjs` today (`claudeMdSize: {chars:11991, budget:12000, withinBudget:true}`) — 9 characters of headroom. CLAUDE.md:11 says "Over budget means split, don't append" and "Correcting a wrong line does not license a longer one". Three junctions notice the wall and propose their line anyway; none reconciles. Without a single arbitration pass an agent will either blow the budget (health + ops-sweep go red) or silently drop pointers, orphaning every file this audit just created.
3. TWO INCOMPATIBLE PROPOSALS FOR THE SAME DIRECTORY, ONE OF WHICH BREAKS A STATED LAW — the `ops namespaces A` and `ops root` junctions both propose creating `session-manager-operations/README.md`; the `nested-md-strategy` junction instead proposes `session-manager-operations/CLAUDE.md` and explicitly moves the SINGLE-WRITER LAW block (CLAUDE.md:80-93, 1,289 bytes) out of root CLAUDE.md into it. The `root` junction's must_not_contain forbids exactly that: "Any restatement of the single-writer OWNERS enumeration outside CLAUDE.md:84-93". Verified neither file exists. An agent working the digest top-to-bottom could create both files and/or relocate the law that CLAUDE.md:83 is cited as the authority for.
4. THREE INCOMPATIBLE SPECS FOR ONE FILE: `session-manager-operations/architecture/README.md` is proposed by arch-a (TIER 1 law-rationale / TIER 2 subsystem reference / TIER 3 unreferenced), arch-b (genre: REFERENCE vs POINT-IN-TIME), and arch-c (index table with a class column REFERENCE/RUNBOOK/FINDINGS-REVIEW), each with a different root_pointer_line. Verified the directory holds 19 entries (18 .md + build-target.json) and no README; ops-sweep emits MISSING_README for it. Whichever agent writes first defines the taxonomy, and the other two must_contain lists then read as missing content and get appended — re-growing the doc this index exists to shrink.
5. A LIVE FOURTH GUARD HOOK IS INVISIBLE TO EVERY JUNCTION, EVERY DOC, AND THE NPM TARBALL — `scripts/hooks/guard-self-schedule.cjs` (tracked, added today, header cites PRD 1204/2026-09-13) is registered in .claude/settings.json:31 on matcher `ScheduleWakeup|CronCreate|Task|Agent`, and `src/main/lib/guardShims.cjs:45` GUARD_NAMES installs a shim for it. But: (a) no junction in the digest mentions it — the scripts junction's proposed invoker table enumerates only three guards and omits the 4th matcher entirely; (b) project-partition.md:67 and :86-87 list only three guards; (c) package.json files[11-13] ships only three, while guardShims writes four shims whose body does `require(<appRoot>/scripts/hooks/<name>)` — so on an npm install the 4th shim points at a file that was never packed; (d) `scripts/__tests__/package-files.test.cjs:38-46,107` hardcodes "The three PreToolUse guard scripts" / "
6. project-partition.md:69 IS ACTIVELY DANGEROUS AND SELF-CERTIFIES AS COMPLETE — "`scripts/lib/` is required only by `scheduler-watchdog.cjs`" while ~10 production modules under src/main require it (index.cjs:77,97; health.cjs:23; scheduler.cjs:176; lib/{queueStore,prdLocations,projectRootResolve,ephemeralCwd,opsOwnership,opsErrorLog,effectiveModelInfo}). src/main/index.cjs cannot boot without it. The same doc's :108-112 certifies "no top-level path is unassigned or duplicated" — falsified twice over: `scripts/sync-settings-schema.cjs` (live, wired to `npm run sync:settings-schema`) and `scripts/hooks/guard-self-schedule.cjs` are both absent from its enumerations. An agent trusting either statement can move or delete scripts/lib and break app boot.
7. LIVE OUTAGE NO DOC RECORDS — `systemctl --user status scheduler-watchdog.service` right now: `Active: failed (Result: exit-code) since Sun 2026-09-13 13:42:15 PDT; 52s ago`, `status=209/STDOUT`, failing on every 2-minute tick because the unit appends stdout to repo-root `logs/`, which does not exist (`ls -d logs` → No such file or directory) while .gitignore:27-30 anchors `/logs/` so it is untracked. The external app-relaunch watchdog has been down since 2026-09-12 20:43. Compounding it, `scripts/scheduler-watchdog.sh:9` tells the reader to run `systemctl --user start sm-scheduler-watchdog.timer` — the installed unit is `scheduler-watchdog`, so the documented command fails with "Unit not found".
8. arch-a WOULD ENCODE AN ALREADY-ANSWERED QUESTION AS AN OPEN ONE, INVITING A DESTRUCTIVE DEDUPE — its architecture/README.md must_contain says to name which copy of project-pages-pipeline.md is canonical "pending the owner's decision", and its open_questions still asks whether the duplication is intentional. web, src-main and arch-c all answered it, and I re-verified: md5 of both = 5b429137bc859c883896ac32147eeb9e; web/project-pages/assets.cjs:30-32 declares SPEC_REL (architecture) → SPEC_COPY_REL (src/main/templates) and :110 writes the copy; publish-gate.cjs gates drift inside prepublishOnly; src/main/lib/projectHomeAdminRoutes.cjs:41 reads the templates copy at runtime to serve project_home_get_contract. Orchestrator lead (1) is REFUTED. Deleting either copy breaks publish and the MCP contract route.
9. THE CI-RED DIAGNOSIS POINTS AT A PATH THAT DOES NOT EXIST — the tests junction states projectHomeAdminRoutes.cjs:79 requires `scripts/project-pages-logic/dist/logic.cjs` and that "the file is absent locally too". Verified: the constant is at line 43, `LOGIC_BUNDLE_PATH = <root>/web/project-pages/logic/dist/logic.cjs`, `scripts/project-pages-logic/` does not exist, and web/project-pages/logic/dist/logic.cjs DOES exist locally. The real hazard is still live but differently shaped: both bundles are gitignored (`git check-ignore -v` → .gitignore:2:dist) yet listed in package.json files[8-10], and they are built only by `prepublishOnly`; CI's `npm run build` (ci.yml:22) is `vite build` alone and runs AFTER `npm run test:unit` (ci.yml:18). An agent chasing the cited path burns a cycle and may "fix" CI by committing gitignored build output.
10. PRD_AUTHORING.md IS FORKED AND THREE DOCS LINK THE WRONG COPY BY ABSOLUTE file:// URL — CLAUDE.md:109, conventions.md:27 and scheduler-operations.md:10 all point at `file:///home/bilko/.claude/session-manager/scheduled-plans/PRD_AUTHORING.md` (30,937 B), unresolvable off this machine, while the repo ships `src/main/templates/PRD_AUTHORING.md` (verified 27,296 B, mtime 2026-08-01). scheduler.cjs:1293 seeds only `if (!fs.existsSync(dest))`, so the copies can never reconverge; the live copy still names the deleted `src/main/adminServer.cjs`. Every PRD-authoring agent on this machine reads the stale fork, and every agent on any other machine reads a dead link.

## Critic — contradictions between junction reports (arbitrated in the plan)

- ops root file, kind AND content: `ops namespaces A` and `ops root` both propose creating `session-manager-operations/README.md` (navigational index, README on purpose); `nested-md-strategy` proposes `session-manager-operations/CLAUDE.md` instead AND moves CLAUDE.md:80-93's SINGLE-WRITER LAW into it to reclaim 1,289 bytes. The `root` junction's must_not_contain explicitly forbids restating the OWNERS enumeration outside CLAUDE.md:84-93. Three junctions, two files, one prohibited move.
- `session-manager-operations/architecture/README.md` is proposed three separate times (arch-a, arch-b, arch-c) with three mutually exclusive taxonomies (linkage tiers 1/2/3 · genre REFERENCE-vs-POINT-IN-TIME · class column REFERENCE/RUNBOOK/FINDINGS-REVIEW) and three different root_pointer_lines. No junction acknowledges the other two.
- Root CLAUDE.md budget: 13 junctions each propose a root_pointer_line (~2,800 chars total) against 9 chars of verified headroom (11,991/12,000). src-renderer, arch-a and nested-md-strategy each flag the wall in open_questions and still emit a line. Nobody proposes which existing lines get cut.
- project-pages-pipeline.md canonicality: web, src-main and arch-c independently establish (and I re-verified) that the src/main/templates copy is a generated, drift-gated artifact of assets.cjs:30-32/:110 + publish-gate.cjs. arch-a still lists it as an open question and would write "pending the owner's decision" into the new architecture README. arch-a also frames the pair as a defect ("no sync mechanism") that nested-md-strategy repeats.
- agent-layer-consumers.md disposition: arch-b says MOVE it to session-manager-operations/reviews/2026-09-12-agent-layer-consumers.md and drop its superseded tail; arch-a says KEEP it in architecture/ and link it from project-partition.md + plugins/CLAUDE.md; nested-md-strategy says link it from root CLAUDE.md's table or plugins/CLAUDE.md. It also has uncommitted WIP, which arch-b notes blocks a move and the others do not.
- global-settings-inventory.md disposition: arch-a says index it in place under a 'point-in-time audit, not maintained' heading; arch-b says move to reviews/ with a dated filename OR distil into a new architecture/settings-storage.md of ~40 lines with no line numbers. Delete-vs-archive-vs-distil is unresolved across the two.
- scheduler-ops-standards.md: arch-c recommends deleting its central section (:44-63) and folding the survivors into scheduler-operations.md, then deleting the file and the pointer at scheduler-operations.md:12-14; arch-a treats it as a legitimate TIER-2 subsystem doc reachable via that exact pointer and proposes indexing it. arch-c also demonstrates the pointer is to a doc that is wrong on the concurrency cap.
- docs/web-remote/: the docs junction says banner it as an archive and leave it in docs/; the web junction says move ARCHITECTURE/ARCHITECTURE-V2-MOBILE/PENTEST/LAUNCH-POINT under web-remote/ and archive PRIOR-ART; nested-md-strategy says the 'legacy' label is contestable because three of those files were committed 2026-09-12 and asks whether they are live WEB PRESENCE docs. Three dispositions, one directory.
- feedback namespace lint status: `ops namespaces A` reports ops-sweep findings [] for feedback (readmeHasRetentionPolicy true); arch-c asserts feedback is in NEITHER opsOwnership.cjs OWNERS nor CLAUDE.md's non-owned enumeration so ops-sweep's UNDOCUMENTED rule fires on it. I ran the sweep: feedback → `mentionedInClaudeMd: true, claudeMdRetiredSignal: true, findings: []`. CLAUDE.md:90 does name it. arch-c is wrong.
- CI-red root cause: the tests junction cites src/main/lib/projectHomeAdminRoutes.cjs:79 → `scripts/project-pages-logic/dist/logic.cjs`, 'absent locally too'. Verified the real constant is line 43 → `web/project-pages/logic/dist/logic.cjs`, and it exists locally. The moved path is exactly the PRD-1184 relocation the web junction documents, so two junctions describe the same file with different paths.
- vitest.config.ts arithmetic: src-main says 247 include entries / 243 explicit / dead entry at :26; tests says 248 / 244 / :28; src-renderer cites the globs at :17-19 and 'tests/unit glob at :18'. Verified programmatically: 248 entries, 4 globs (tests/unit/**/*.spec.ts, src/renderer/**/*.test.ts, *.test.tsx, *.spec.ts), 244 literals, exactly 1 dangling literal, at line 28.
- The vitest hand-registration law is claimed by two proposed files at once: tests/README.md must_contain states it as 'the law with its enforcement' and argues a tests/CLAUDE.md would be wrong because it would not auto-load when editing src/main/__tests__; src/main/CLAUDE.md must_contain states the same law for the same reason. Both would ship on day one with a duplicated law and no stated owner.
- The scripts/lib inversion fact is slated for three homes simultaneously — scripts/README.md ('treat lib/ as main-process runtime, not tooling'), src/main/CLAUDE.md ('KNOWN INVERSION' with 10 call sites), and a correction to project-partition.md:69 — with three junctions each assuming theirs is the place it lands.
- src/seed/ direction: src-main asks whether src/seed/prompts should be dropped from package.json files (already inlined by promptsSeed.ts's eager glob — note src-renderer separately lists promptsSeed.ts as a DEAD module with zero importers, which undercuts that reasoning); agent-layer asks whether src/seed/agents should be reclassified out of DESKTOP HARNESS into AGENT LAYER; nested-md-strategy treats src/seed as settled shipped content. Three junctions, three directions.

- **bin/ — a shipped npm entrypoint with no owning junction** — bin/cli.cjs is the FIRST code every npx user executes and is package.json files[0]. No junction claimed it, and none of the proposed new docs covers it, so it stays documented by a single clause in conventions.md:61.

  - checked: CONFIRMED. Both trees are shipped whole (package.json files[16,17]). The gap is a decision, not a fact: pick src/CLAUDE.md or two paragraphs, but do not leave api.d.ts's mirror rule homeless.

So roughly 39% of the operations state is on one machine only, and it is all permanent `git status` noise. ## Confirmed stale claims, grouped by document (200)

### .claude/agents/builder.md (1)

- **.claude/agents/builder.md:10-11** [cosmetic/cosmetic]
  - claim: `~/.claude/agents/builder.md` is machine-local and not version-controlled in any repo — it lives outside git entirely.
  - actual: Not-version-controlled is correct, but 'outside git entirely' is not: `/home/bilko/.claude` IS a git work tree, and `agents/` is excluded by that repo's own .gitignore rather than being outside any repo. The practical conclusion the paragraph draws (changes there won't survive a machine rebuild) still holds; only the mechanism stated is wrong.
  - replacement: Note: `~/.claude/agents/builder.md` is machine-local and untracked — `~/.claude` is a git repo, but its allowlist `.gitignore` excludes `agents/`, so that file is never committed anywhere.

### .claude/agents/project-home-builder.md (1)

- **.claude/agents/project-home-builder.md:3** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: description: Generates session-manager's 5 static Project Page HTML files ... from the saved component library and a computed project summary.
  - actual: The same file's body forbids treating the saved component library as a source: pages are generated from the live MCP contract's catalog, and the saved design-mock library is explicitly read-only history. The frontmatter `description` is what the Agent Library UI and `composeEpicIntake` fold into an Epic's opening prompt, so the contradicted line is the one a
  - replacement: description: Generates session-manager's 5 static Project Page HTML files (Home / Marketing Landing / Feature Description / Architecture Overview / Brief) by following the app's own MCP contract (`project_home_get_contract`) — its live component catalog plus a computed project summary.

### .gitignore (1)

- **.gitignore:15-16** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: !.claude/agents/builder.md / !.claude/agents/project-home-builder.md (the only two agent personas un-ignored)
  - actual: .claude/agents/bilko-host-publisher.md exists on disk and is an in-use persona — project-partition.md:15 names `bilko-host-publisher` as a WEB PRESENCE consumer — but it is untracked, so no clone, worktree, or headless scheduler run gets it.
  - replacement: .gitignore should also carry `!.claude/agents/bilko-host-publisher.md` alongside the existing `!.claude/agents/builder.md` and `!.claude/agents/project-home-builder.md` un-ignore lines, so all three in-use personas reach clones/worktrees/headless runs.

### CLAUDE.md (10)

- **CLAUDE.md:30** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: "Electron 33 (CommonJS main + preload) · React 18 + Vite · Tailwind · zustand · xterm + node-pty"
  - actual: Electron is pinned at 42.1.0. Same defect as README.md:116 — the version string is duplicated in both docs and both are wrong.
  - replacement: Electron 42 (CommonJS main + preload) · React 18 + Vite · Tailwind · zustand · xterm + node-pty · Whisper
- **CLAUDE.md:41** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: "`npm run lint` — `lint:selectors` + `lint:hooks`. Both guard blank-screen crashes; run with typecheck."
  - actual: `lint` now chains three sub-lints; the third, `lint:unregistered-tests`, guards the 268-line manual include list in vitest.config.ts and has nothing to do with blank-screen crashes.
  - replacement: - `npm run lint` — `lint:selectors` + `lint:hooks` (both guard blank-screen crashes) + `lint:unregistered-tests` (catches test files missing from vitest.config.ts's manual `include` list); run with typecheck.
- **CLAUDE.md:43** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: "`npm publish` — runs `vite build` via `prepublishOnly`. Tag `latest`."
  - actual: prepublishOnly runs four steps: the project-pages renderer build, the project-pages logic build, the publish gate, and only then vite build. A failing project-pages gate now blocks publish — a fact the line hides.
  - replacement: - `npm publish` — `prepublishOnly` runs `build:project-pages` + `build:project-pages-logic` + `project-pages:gate` (exits 1 on stale bundles/catalog/spec copy, blocking publish) then `vite build`. Tag `latest`.
- **CLAUDE.md:90** [cosmetic/cosmetic]
  - claim: "`browser` is a leftover, safe to delete on sight."
  - actual: session-manager-operations/browser no longer exists; the instruction is a no-op referring to an already-deleted directory.

- **README.md:71, 86, 108 (table row "| F1 | Push-to-talk |"), 105-112** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: "Press F7 to open the microphone setup wizard, then F1 to start dictating."; "Press F1 for push-to-talk."; keyboard table rows `F1 | Push-to-talk` and `F7 | Microphone setup wizard`
  - actual: The default push-to-talk accelerator is `Cmd+Option+V` on darwin and `Ctrl+Shift+Space` elsewhere — and a bare `F1` is not even a legal accelerator, since parseAccelerator rejects any string with fewer than two '+'-separated parts. 'F7' appears nowhere as a key binding; it is only a PRD codename in comments for the first-run mic wizard, which auto-triggers r
  - replacement: Push-to-talk defaults to Cmd+Option+V on macOS and Ctrl+Shift+Space on Linux/Windows (rebindable in the Voice tab); the first-run microphone wizard has no hotkey — it auto-arms on first launch and opens on the first mic click.
- **README.md:111 (table row "| Ctrl-Shift-R | Reboot active session (preset-aware) |")** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: Ctrl-Shift-R reboots the active session
  - actual: CmdOrCtrl+Shift+R restarts the whole Session Manager application. Restarting the terminal in the active tab is CmdOrCtrl+Shift+S, which the README omits entirely.
  - replacement: | Ctrl-Shift-R | Restart Session Manager app | and add | Ctrl-Shift-S | Restart terminal in active tab |
- **README.md:112 (table row "| Ctrl-Shift-B | Toggle build mode for active tab |")** [misleads-an-agent/misleads-an-agent]
  - claim: Ctrl-Shift-B toggles build mode for the active tab
  - actual: There is no 'build mode' feature anywhere in the tree, and no Ctrl-Shift-B keydown handler. The app's own in-product learning copy says Ctrl+Shift+B opens the Broadcast bar, and BroadcastBar is driven by state (`setBroadcastOpen`), not by any registered keyboard shortcut.
  - replacement: | (Command Palette only) | Broadcast one prompt to several tabs — no keyboard shortcut is registered; open it via Cmd-K / Ctrl-K |

  - actual: No Ctrl-W binding exists — not in the app menu template, not in the single window keydown handler, not in TabBar.tsx.
  - replacement: Delete the "| Ctrl-W | Close tab |" row — there is no keyboard shortcut for closing a tab; a session tab is closed only by clicking the X on the tab (src/renderer/components/TabBar.tsx:163).

- Recommendation: **create CLAUDE.md session-manager-operations/CLAUDE.md** — Three forces converge on this one file. (1) Discovery: a nested CLAUDE.md auto-loads exactly when a file in its subtree enters context, which is precisely when the single-writer law becomes relevant — today those 14 lines are paid for on every turn of every session including ones that never touch the ops folder. (2) Budget: root is at 11,991 of 12,000 bytes (verified `wc -c`), 9 bytes of headroom, and health.cjs:856-873 + ops-sweep.cjs:145-151 both enforce it; moving CLAUDE.md:80-93 frees 1,289 bytes, the single largest coherent block, and buys room to FIX the four confirmed root-level stale claims (tab count, Electron version, PRD_AUTHORING pointer, telemetry-vs-README) that currently canno

  - `## What's here` — 12 namespaces: architecture, bilko-host, design-mocks, feedback, HUMAN_LEARN, logs, manual, project-brief, project-pages, prompt-sessions, reviews, scheduler (verified by `ls`; ops-sweep reports namespaceCount 12)
  - The SINGLE-WRITER LAW block moved verbatim from CLAUDE.md:80-93 (1,289 bytes): the OWNERS enumeration, the deliberately-NOT-owned list, the feedback-retired note, and the 'any new top-level folder must land in this enumeration or in OWNERS in the same PR' rule
  - `## What must NOT be assumed` — do not write to an owned namespace outside its owner (src/main/lib/opsOwnership.cjs throws fail-closed); do not build ops paths by string concat, use opsPath(); do not add a namespace without also adding it here, or scripts/ops-sweep.cjs flags UNDOCUMENTED
  - One line: every namespace dir must carry its own README.md or `node scripts/ops-sweep.cjs <cwd>` emits MISSING_README (scripts/ops-sweep.cjs:187) — currently red for architecture/, design-mocks/, HUMAN_LEARN/, reviews/
